# Seed 38 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the new selection's first run: small (Iteration 151)

`seed 38 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier
--cpus 2,8`, commit 562c4d18 (Iteration 129), started 14:25: the fronts of
seeds 33, 35, 36 and 37 carried in (695 designs), four workers (cpus 2, 4, 8,
12), population 256, 83 generations before the deadline (4 h 42 min of
evolution), 21,395 designs, 20,396 alive; 999 refused by the reach limit - no
other death. The first run of the selection with bubble and ackermann (the
owner's RelF benchmarks, Iterations 127-128). The chain took 6 h 49 min: the
seed pack 5 h 01 min in (the front re-measured in 18 min 39 s), the comparison
1 h 47 min - the longer workloads. Session 33 measured it with seeds 33, 35, 36
and 37 (calibration 1.008; cpu 2 1.008, cpu 8 1.004), every front under the
new selection.

**Small.** Against the best of the earlier runs in the session:

| objective | seed 38 | seeds 33-37, best | |
|---|---|---|---|
| fastest | 0.236 (cpu 2) | 0.241 (seed 37) | 2.1% on cpu 2 - but on cpu 8 seed 37's 38446e6c09 is the fastest of all (0.232): a tie within the two CPUs' disagreement |
| smallest binary + image | 20,622 | 20,630 (seed 37) | 0.04% smaller, under 2% |
| least memory | 152 KB | 156 KB (seed 37) | 4 KB less, within the 8 KB tie |

**On the front of all runs (88): 69 of seed 38** - against 17 of seed 37, one
each of seeds 33 and 35, none of seed 36: the run fitted the new workloads and
moved no objective's best.

Its own front, 165 designs: fastest d5f76f0d30 (0.231 re-measured, 33,704
bytes, 220 KB), smallest a1acc93b92 (20,622 bytes, speed 1.358), least memory
05d6ba6a35 (152 KB, speed 0.384). The genes on it: bss and lean on all 165,
kfast 162, kinput 146, fbuf 112, rtfuse 87, tracer 67, guard 61, abortif 53,
the JIT 47 (jitx 6), d256 40, kstack 16, opcall 11.

**Held-out check** (Iteration 150's code, on the VM): the 165 front designs as
s6 on all 14 files of the Forth 2012 suite - none differs.

Next: seed 39, the same genes and measurement - the pair the measurement
change asked for; then the after-seed-39 batch (seven pending patches).
